00022: def smooth(points,steps=5):
00023:     p=[Vector(points[0])]+[Vector(q) for q in points]+[Vector(points[-1])]
00024:     out=[]
00025:     for i in range(1,len(p)-2):
00026:         a,b,c,d=p[i-1:i+3]
00027:         for j in range(steps):
00028:             t=j/steps
00029:             v=.5*((2*b)+(-a+c)*t+(2*a-5*b+4*c-d)*t*t+
00030:                   (-a+3*b-3*c+d)*t*t*t)
00031:             q=tuple(v)
00032:             if not out or (Vector(q)-Vector(out[-1])).length>1e-7:
00033:                 out.append(q)
00034:     q=tuple(p[-2])
00035:     if (Vector(q)-Vector(out[-1])).length>1e-7:
00036:         out.append(q)
00037:     return out
00038:

00264: def softbox(name,center,radii,mat,exponents=(.34,.45),rotation=(0,0,0)):
